ElementTree uses '' for default namespace\n        if prefix in ns_map and ns_map[prefix] == uri:\n            continue\n        ns_map[prefix] = uri\n\n    for prefix, uri in ns_map.items():\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix if prefix is not None else \"\", uri)\n\n    return ns_map\n\n\ndef iter_text_nodes_in_para(p: ET.Element) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    # Text nodes are <hp:t> elements nested under <hp:run> (often), but to be safe\n    # we collect any element with localname 't' under this paragraph.\n    return [el for el in p.iter() if localname(el.tag) == \"t\"]\n\n\ndef remove_lineseg_caches(p: ET.Element) -> None:\n    # We remove both forms.\n    targets = {\"linesegarray\", \"lineseg\", \"lineSegArray\", \"lineSeg\"}\n    # Only remove direct children of <hp:p> to avoid accidentally removing\n    # unrelated \"High\": \"즉시조치\",\n        \"Medium\": \"계획보완\",\n        \"Low\": \"모니터링\",\n    }\n    return mapping.get(tier, \"\")\n\n\ndef build_replacements(overview: dict, actions: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, str], str, str]:\n    summary = overview.get(\"summary\", {})\n\n    replacements: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in summary.items():\n        if isinstance(v, (str, int, float)):\n < len(immediate) else \"\"\n\n    raw_date = str(summary.get(\"점검일\", \"\"))\n    dotted_date = DATE_RE.sub(r\"\\1.\\2.\\3\", raw_date) if raw_date else \"\"\n\n    risk_tier = str(summary.get(\"위험등급\", \"\"))\n\n    return replacements, raw_date, dotted_date, risk_tier\n\n\ndef replace_in_paragraph(p: ET.Element, replacements: dict[str, str], risk_tier: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if paragraph text was modified.\"\"\"\n    t_nodes = iter_text_nodes_in_para(p)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n\n    merged = \"\".join([(n.text or \"\") for n in t_nodes])\n    if \"{{\" not in merged and \"}}\" not in merged:\n        return False\n\n    def repl(match: re.Match) -> str:\n        key = match.group(1)\n        if key == \"위험등급\":\n            note = severity_note_for_tier(risk_tier)\n            if note:\n                return f\"{risk_tier} {note}\"\n            return risk_tier\n        return replacements.get(key, match.group(0))\n\n = replaced\n    for n in t_nodes[1:]:\n        n.text = \"\"\n\n    remove_lineseg_caches(p)\n    return True\n\n\ndef rewrite_dates_everywhere(root: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Rewrite any YYYY-MM-DD literals to YYYY.MM.DD in all <...:t> nodes.\"\"\"\n    changed = 0\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) != \"t\":\n            continue\n        if not el.text:\n Text nodes are <hp:t> elements nested under <hp:run> (often), but to be safe\n    # we collect any element with localname 't' under this paragraph.\n    return [el for el in p.iter() if localname(el.tag) == \"t\"]\n\n\ndef remove_lineseg_caches(p: ET.Element) -> None:\n    # HWPX sometimes uses either camelCase (lineSegArray/lineSeg) or lowercase\n    # (linesegarray/lineseg). We remove both forms.\n    targets = {\"linesegarray\", \"lineseg\", \"lineSegArray\", \"lineSeg\"}\n    # Only remove direct children of <hp:p> to avoid accidentally removing\n    # unrelated \"\"\"Return True if paragraph text was modified.\"\"\"\n    t_nodes = iter_text_nodes_in_para(p)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n\n    merged = \"\".join([(n.text or \"\") for n in note = severity_note_for_tier(risk_tier)\n            if note:\n                return f\"{risk_tier} {note}\"\n            return risk_tier\n        return replacements.get(key, match.group(0))\n\n
